===== profile-runner.txt =====

from pathlib import Path
import subprocess,json,time,sys,selectors,hashlib
p=Path(sys.argv[1]);peer=sys.argv[2];exe=p/'vector-costs-profile';result={'peer':peer,'child_command':[str(exe),'growth-api-profile',peer],'started_unix':time.time(),'image_sha256_before':hashlib.sha256(exe.read_bytes()).hexdigest()};start=time.monotonic()
with (p/(peer+'-child.stderr')).open('w') as err, (p/(peer+'-child.stdout')).open('w') as out:
 child=subprocess.Popen(result['child_command'],stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=err,text=True);result['child_pid']=child.pid
 sel=selectors.DefaultSelector();sel.register(child.stdout,selectors.EVENT_READ);ready=sel.select(10);sel.close()
 first=child.stdout.readline() if ready else '';out.write(first);out.flush();result['readiness']=first.rstrip();result['readiness_ok']=first.startswith('profile-ready ')
 if result['readiness_ok']:
  cmd=['/usr/bin/sample',str(child.pid),'60','1','-mayDie','-file',str(p/(peer+'-sample.txt'))];result['sample_command']=cmd;sample_start=time.monotonic()
  with (p/(peer+'-sample.stdout')).open('w') as so,(p/(peer+'-sample.stderr')).open('w') as se:
   sampler=subprocess.Popen(cmd,stdout=so,stderr=se);result['sample_pid']=sampler.pid;(p/(peer+'-profile-status.json')).write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n');print('owned child',child.pid,'sampler',sampler.pid,flush=True);result['sample_exit']=sampler.wait()
  result['sample_seconds']=time.monotonic()-sample_start
 else:
  result['sample_exit']=None;result['sample_not_launched']='readiness not observed; no retry'
 rest=child.stdout.read();out.write(rest);result['child_exit']=child.wait()
result.update({'seconds':time.monotonic()-start,'ended_unix':time.time(),'image_sha256_after':hashlib.sha256(exe.read_bytes()).hexdigest()});(p/(peer+'-profile-status.json')).write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(result),flush=True)
sys.exit(0 if result['child_exit']==0 and result.get('sample_exit')==0 and result['image_sha256_before']==result['image_sha256_after'] else 1)

Interpretation: scalar-body CFA=SP+80, saved LR=CFA-8 and FP=CFA-16 match the native prologue. The malloc/memcpy/free return PCs 0x1000114cc/0x1000114e4/0x1000114ec fall under this rule. The function saves x29 but does not establish a new x29 frame chain. The FDE has no intermediate prologue/epilogue rows, so asynchronous boundary precision is a separate limitation; absent body unwind metadata is not an explanation for lost caller ancestry at these settled call-return PCs.

Local documentation command: MANPAGER=cat man sample | col -b. It describes suspension and recorded call stacks but does not specify use of DWARF versus compact unwind versus frame-chain walking. Therefore sample unwinder policy is unresolved.

Current emission: compiler/src/backend/emitter.rs:550 emits group 0 with only probe-stack. Despite no explicit uwtable attribute, unwind sections and linked FDEs already exist. No metadata-only fix is established. A future explicit-frame-pointer diagnostic changes native instructions and cannot be treated as an unchanged-code timing comparison; an alternate unwinder or a known frameless C control could discriminate tool policy. Neither was run here.
